## Transcripts

### greet-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 你好，你能帮我做什么？
  [partner message] 我是 Ducter。比如你可以让我修好某个仓库里挂掉的测试。
```

### greet-en

```
--- step 1: user says: hi! what can you do?
  [partner message] I'm Ducter, your personal AI partner. For example, I can get a broken test in a repo fixed while you're doing something else.
```

### greet-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 안녕하세요. 오늘 어떤 일을 도와줄 수 있나요?
  [partner message] 안녕하세요! 저는 Ducter예요. 이 컴퓨터에서 할 수 있는 일을 맡길 수 있어요. 예를 들어 "이 프로젝트에서 실패하는 테스트 고쳐줘"처럼 말씀하시면 제가 작업을 열어서 끝나면 알려드릴게요.
```

### ok-keeps-ko

```
--- step 1: user says: 고마워요. 내일 아침에 다시 얘기해요.
  [partner message] 아니요, 고마워요. 내일 아침에 봐요.
--- step 2: user says: OK
  [partner message] 네, 내일 아침에 봐요. 편히 쉬어요.
```

### direct-ja

```
--- step 1: user says: 一言で：TCP と UDP の一番の違いは何ですか？
  [partner message] TCPは「届くこと」を保証し、UDPは「速さ」を優先します。

TCPは届いたか確認し、欠けたら再送し、順番も揃えて渡してくれます。UDPはそれをせず、送りっぱなしで軽く速いです。
```

### direct-es

```
--- step 1: user says: En dos líneas: ¿cuándo debo hacer rebase en vez de merge?
  [partner message] Usa rebase para actualizar tu rama privada con los últimos cambios de la principal y dejar un historial lineal antes de compartirla. Usa merge cuando la rama ya es compartida o cuando quieres conservar tal cual cómo se desarrolló el trabajo.
```

### direct-zh

```
--- step 1: user says: 一句话：TCP 和 UDP 最大的区别是什么？
  [partner message] TCP 面向连接、保证可靠送达和顺序；UDP 无连接、不保证，但开销小、速度快。
```

### direct-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two lines: when should I rebase instead of merge?
  [partner message] Rebase your own unshared branch to tidy its commits onto the latest base before it's reviewed. Merge when others already have the branch or you want the history to show exactly when the work was integrated.
```

### direct-zh-howto

```
--- step 1: user says: bun test 怎么只跑一个测试文件？
  [partner message] 直接把文件路径跟在后面就行：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts
```

如果只记得文件名的一部分，可以只写关键词，bun 会匹配路径里包含它的测试文件：

```bash
bun test foo
```

想只跑某个测试用例，用 `-t` 按名字过滤：

```bash
bun test test/foo.test.ts -t "某个用例的名字"
```

### mem-source-none-en

```
--- step 1: user says: In two sentences: what's a write-ahead log for?
  [partner message] A write-ahead log is a file where every change to the database is written down before it's applied to the main data. If the system crashes midway, the log lets it replay or undo those changes so no committed work is lost and the data stays consistent.
```

```
(earlier) user: 每天早上帮我看一下周五北京到上海的高铁票价，600 以下的告诉我。
(earlier) partner ack: 好，每天早上去看一次。  → task "每天看周五京沪高铁票价" working; its report: 第 3 天：周五 G1 553 元（二等座余 12 张），G3 553 元（余 4 张），G7 626 元；600 以下的有 2 班。
(earlier) partner: 周五 600 以下的还有两班：G1 和 G3，都是 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。
(earlier) Ducter offered on its own card: keep task "fares"'s rounds on a board?
--- step 1: user answers the open decision: go
  [partner message] 做好了，周五京沪高铁的看板已经建好：600 元以下的是 G1 和 G3，各 553 元，G3 只剩 4 张。之后每天的新票价会自动更新到这里。
  [an app card under the message: made "京沪高铁票价看板", with Open] the app's file now (v1, 1748 characters): <main class="k-app">
  <section class="k-sum">
    <div class="k-sum-label">周五 北京南 → 上海虹桥</div>
    <div class="k-sum-value" id="cnt">—</div>
    <div class="k-sum-sub" id="sub">600 元以下的班次</div>
  </section>
  <h2 class="k-h">二等座票价</h2>
  <div class="k-card">
    <div class="k-list" id="list"></div>
  </div>
</main>
<script>
(function () {
  var LIMIT = 600;
  var defaults = ducter.get("defaults") || [];
  function esc(s) {
    return String(s).replace(/[&<>"']/g, function (c) {
      return { "&": "&amp;", "<": "&lt;", ">": "&gt;", '"': "&quot;", "'": "&#39;" }[c];
    });
  }
  function render(data) {
    var fares = (data && data.fares) || defaults;
    var cheap = fares.filter(function (f) { return f.price < LIMIT; });
    document.getElementById("cnt").textContent = cheap.length + " 班";
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = cheap.length
      ? cheap.map(function (f) { return f.no + " " + f.price + " 元"; }).join("、") + "，600 元以下"
      : "暂时没有 600 元以下的班次";
    document.getElementById("list").innerHTML = fares.map(function (f) {
      var left = f.seats == null ? "余票未知" : "余 " + f.seats + " 张";
      var cls = f.price < LIMIT ? "k-row-value k-good" : "k-row-value";
      return '<div class="k-row"><div class="k-row-main">' + esc(f.no) +
        '<div class="k-row-sub">' + esc(left) + '</div></div>' +
        '<div class="' + cls + '">¥' + esc(f.price) + '</div></div>';
    }).join("");
    ducter.summary(cheap.length ? "周五 600 元以下 " + cheap.length + " 班" : "周五暂无 600 元以下");
  }
  var stored = ducter.get("fares");
  render(stored ? { fares: stored.fares || stored } : null);
  ducter.onChange(function () {
    var d = ducter.get("fares");
    render(d ? { fares: d.fares || d } : null);
  });
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"fares":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}],"defaults":[{"no":"G1","price":553,"seats":12},{"no":"G3","price":553,"seats":4},{"no":"G7","price":626,"seats":null}]}
  [Ducter passed the user's answer to task fares] [Ducter] The person now keeps this work's rounds in their small app "京沪高铁票价看板" (app ac035e804). Each round, write what stands now into it with app_data (app ac035e804, set {key: value}; null removes a key); the app reads the keys defaults, fares. Write whole values (a key's value is replaced), once a round. Then don't report_to_main for a round that only updated it: report only when something need
  [also used tools] app
```

</div>

  <summary>记一笔</summary>

  <div class="k-field"><label>金额（元）</label><input class="k-num" id="yuan" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="0"></div>

<script>
function draw(){
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  const total = xs.reduce((s, x) => s + Number(x.yuan || 0), 0);
  document.getElementById("total").textContent = "¥" + total;
  document.getElementById("count").textContent = xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔" : "还没记";
  const list = document.getElementById("list");
  list.innerHTML = "";
  for (const x of xs) {
    const row = document.createElement("div");
    row.className = "k-row";
    row.innerHTML = '<div class="k-row-main"></div><div class="k-row-sub"></div><div class="k-row-value"></div>';
    row.querySelector(".k-row-main").textContent = x.what;
    row.querySelector(".k-row-sub").textContent = "";
    row.querySelector(".k-row-value").textContent = "¥" + x.yuan;
    list.appendChild(row);
  }
  if (!xs.length) {
    const e = document.createElement("div");
    e.className = "k-empty";
    e.textContent = "还没记。记下的花销会列在这里。";
    list.appendChild(e);
  }
  ducter.summary(xs.length ? xs.length + " 笔,合计 ¥" + total : "还没记");
}
document.getElementById("save").onclick = () => {
  const what = document.getElementById("what").value.trim();
  const yuan = Number(document.getElementById("yuan").value);
  const day = document.getElementById("day").value.trim() || new Date().toISOString().slice(0, 10);
  if (!what || !yuan) return;
  const xs = ducter.get("expenses") || [];
  xs.push({ what, yuan, day });
  ducter.set("expenses", xs);
  document.getElementById("what").value = "";
  document.getElementById("yuan").value = "";
  document.getElementById("day").value = "";
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();
</script>
</main>; its data now (what it opens with): {"expenses":[{"what":"高铁","yuan":662,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"酒店两晚","yuan":1036,"day":"2026-11-18"},{"what":"午饭","yuan":268,"day":"2026-11-19"}]}

<script>
(function () {
  var KEY = 'feeds';
  var sel = null;

  function pad(n) { return (n < 10 ? '0' : '') + n; }
  function dayKey(d) { return d.getFullYear() + '-' + pad(d.getMonth() + 1) + '-' + pad(d.getDate()); }
  function feeds() { return ducter.get(KEY) || []; }
  function sortKey(f) { return f.d + ' ' + f.t; }

  function render … ).addEventListener('click', function (e) {
    var del = e.target.closest('[data-del]');
    if (del) {
      var id = del.getAttribute('data-del');
      ducter.set(KEY, feeds().filter(function (f) { return f.id !== id; }));
      sel = null;
      render();
      return;
    }
    var row = e.target.closest('.k-row');
    if (row) {
      var id2 = row.getAttribute('data-id');
      sel = sel === id2 ? null : id2;
      render();
    }
  });

  document.getElementById('add').addEventListener('click', function () {
    var tEl = document.getElementById('t');
    var mlEl = document.getElementById('ml');
    var t = tEl.value.trim();
    var m = /^(\d{1,2}):(\d{2})$/.exec(t);
    var ml = Number(mlEl.value);
    if (!m || Number(m[1]) > 23 || Number(m[2]) > 59 || !(ml > 0)) return;
    var now = new Date();
    var rec = {
      id: String(Date.now()) + Math.random().toString(36).slice(2, 6),
      d: dayKey(now),
      t: pad(Number(m[1])) + ':' + m[2],
      ml: ml
    };
    ducter.set(KEY, feeds().concat([rec]));
    tEl.value = '';
    mlEl.value = '';
    document.querySelector('.k-add').open = false;
    render();
  });

  ducter.onChange(render);
  render();
})();
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"feeds":[{"id":"f1","d":"2026-11-20","t":"06:10","ml":120},{"id":"f2","d":"2026-11-20","t":"09:05","ml":90}]}

    list.innerHTML = "";
    var left = 0;
    items.forEach(function (it) {
      var isLanded = !!landed[it.id];
      if (!isLanded) left++;
      var row = document.createElement("div");
      row.className = "k-row" + (isLanded ? " is-landed" : "");
      row.innerHTML =
        '<div class="k-row-main">' +
          '<div>' + it.pr + ' · ' + it.what + '</div>' +
          '<div class="k-row-sub">' + it.who + '</div>' +
        '</div>' +
        '<button class="k-chip" aria-pressed="' + isLanded + '">' + (isLanded ? "Landed" : "Mark landed") + '</button>';
      row.querySelector("button").addEventListener("click", function () {
        landed[it.id] = !landed[it.id];
        save();
        render();
      });
      list.appendChild(row);
    });
    document.getElementById("left").textContent = left;
    document.getElementById("sub").textContent = left === 0
      ? "all landed"
      : (items.length - left) + " of " + items.length + " landed";
    var text = left === 0 ? "All landed" : left + " waiting";
    if (window.ducter && ducter.summary) ducter.summary(text);
  }

  function save() {
    if (window.ducter && ducter.set) ducter.set("landed", landed);
  }

  if (window.ducter && ducter.get) {
    Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
      landed = v || {};
      render();
    }, function () { render(); });
  } else {
    render();
  }
  if (window.ducter && ducter.onChange) {
    ducter.onChange(function () {
      Promise.resolve(ducter.get("landed")).then(function (v) {
        landed = v || {};
        render();
      });
    });
  }
</script>; its data now (what it opens with): {"items":[{"id":"ducter-412","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#412","what":"Compaction cut","who":"Waiting on Weizhen's review since Monday"},{"id":"mem-10287","pr":"nowledge-co/mem#10287","what":"Retry on 502","who":"CI is failing"},{"id":"hawdb-1592","pr":"nowledge-co/hawdb#1592","what":"Flaky compaction test","who":"Waiting on Alice since Tuesday"},{"id":"ducter-418","pr":"wey-gu/ducter#418","what":"Apps bar","who":"Draft, not yet asked for review"}]}
  [also used tools] app
```

<st … )=>`<div class="k-row${i===sel?" sel":""}" data-i="${i}"><div class="k-row-main">${esc(x.note||x.cat)}</div><div class="k-row-value">${money(x.amt)}</div><div class="k-row-sub"><span>${esc(x.cat)}</span><span>${esc(x.day)}</span><span>${esc(x.trip)}</span></div></div>`).join("") : `<p class="k-empty">还没有花销，展开下面的「记一笔」。</p>`;

}

  const amt=parseFloat($("amt").value);
  if(!amt){return}
  const xs=items();
  xs.push({trip:$("trip").value.trim(),day:$("day").value.trim(),cat,amt,note:$("note").value.trim()});
  ducter.set("items",xs);
  $("amt").value="";$("note").value="";
  draw();
};
ducter.onChange(draw);
draw();

```

```

(earlier) partner ack: 我去看看 foo 里那个时好时坏的 TTL 测试，弄清楚了告诉你。  → task "foo: TTL 测试不稳定" done; its report: The TTL test waited on real time for a 1s expiry; under load it fired early. Switched it to a fake clock; 20 runs in a row pass.
(earlier) partner: TTL 测试修好了：它用真实计时去等 1 秒过期，机器一忙就抢跑，现在改成假时钟。
(earlier) user: 再给 ~/dev/foo 的 Cache 加一个 values() 方法
(earlier) partner ack: 我去给 foo 的 Cache 加 values()。  → task "foo: 给 Cache 加 values()" done; its report: Added Cache.values() returning live values; 2 new tests, 14/14 pass.
(earlier) partner: 加好了：values() 只返回没过期的值，加了 2 个测试。
--- step 1: user replies (by swipe, quoting it) to the earlier message with "TTL 测试修好了": 这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻
  [partner ack line] 好，我让它再补一条测试，专门覆盖正好到期那一刻，弄完告诉你。
  [sent to task ttl] 用户说：「这个再补一条测试，覆盖正好到期那一刻」

在 TTL 测试的基础上，再补一条测试：用假时钟把时间推到正好等于过期时间的那一刻，确认此时值已经视为过期（或按现有约定的边界行为，比如 expiresAt <= now 即过期，要和实现保持一致）。同时确认到期前一毫秒仍然有效。

完成标准：新测试通过；整个 TTL 测试文件连跑多次都稳定通过；在报告里贴出测试输出和边界的判断依据。

不在范围内：不要改已有测试的断言，不要合并或推送，只在它现在所在的分支上提交。
```
